# Records Closed on October 6, 2026

A read-only verification checked every open record against commit `3e1445a2d`. It closed these records. "Fixed" means the problem is gone. "Obsolete" means the code is gone or an owner decision replaced the record. "Wrong" means the record's claim was false or applying its fix causes harm. The original text of each record is in the git history of its source file.

203 records were closed: 182 fixed, 17 obsolete, and 4 wrong.
